SELECT [c].[ID], [c].[AcceptableCapacityIncrease], [c].[AcceptableСapacityReduction], [c].[ComponentName], [c].[DocID], [c].[Kind_ID], [c].[ManufacturerName_ID], [c].[MaxCapacity], [c].[MaxOperatingTemperature], [c].[MaxVoltage], [c].[MinCapacity], [c].[MinOperatingTemperature], [c].[MinVoltage], [c].[OutputType], [c].[QualicationSG], [c].[QualicationЕС], [c].[Remark1], [c].[Remark2], [c].[Type_ID], [c0].[RuComponentKind], [c1].[RuComponentType], [c1].[EnComponentType], [m].[ManufacturerName]
FROM [Capacitors] AS [c]

LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m] ON [c].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m].[ID]
